=== .cache/sessions-phase0/confirm-r12-dd.py ===
from pathlib import Path
import os, subprocess, time, datetime, hashlib
out=Path('.cache/sessions-phase0').resolve(); env=dict(os.environ,CGO_ENABLED='0',GOTOOLCHAIN='local',GOAMD64='v1',GOMAXPROCS='16')
pattern='^Benchmark(ObfuscateSQL|SketchAdd|SketchAddMany1k|ObfuscateTraces1k|SketchAddVaried|ObfuscateSQLVaried|SketchAddNegativeBins)$'
start=time.monotonic()
print('Independent confirmation of small negative scalar holdout; unchanged frozen binaries; all seven metrics retained; same isolation/flags,500ms,7alternating pairs',flush=True)
for label in ['baseline','r12']: print(label,hashlib.sha256((out/f'dd-{label}.test').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),flush=True)
for rep in range(1,8):
    for label in (['r12','baseline'] if rep%2 else ['baseline','r12']):
        args=[str(out/f'dd-{label}.test'),'-test.run=^$','-test.bench='+pattern,'-test.benchtime=500ms','-test.count=1','-test.benchmem']
        with (out/f'confirm-r12-dd-{label}-{rep}.txt').open('w') as log:
            log.write('# UTC '+datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()+'\n# '+' '.join(args)+'\n# GOMAXPROCS16 CGO0 Go1.26.8 GOAMD64v1 PGOoff; isolated unchanged binaries\n');log.flush()
            subprocess.run(args,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
        print(label,rep,'elapsed',round(time.monotonic()-start,1),flush=True)
print('CONFIRMATION COMPLETE',flush=True)

=== .cache/sessions-phase0/confirm-r12-stream.py ===
import os, subprocess, time, hashlib, datetime, re
from pathlib import Path
root=Path.cwd(); out=root/'.cache/sessions-phase0'
env=dict(os.environ,CGO_ENABLED='0',GOTOOLCHAIN='local',GOAMD64='v1',GOMAXPROCS='16')
start=time.monotonic()
def sha(path): return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
print('UTC',datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),flush=True)
print('qualification_base_commit',subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],text=True).strip(),flush=True)
print('Go1.26.8 CGO0 GOAMD64v1 PGOoff defaultGOGC/GOMEMLIMIT; noaffinity; benchmark-isolated tools and agents idle',flush=True)
for name in ['dd-baseline.test','dd-r12.test','stream-baseline-r12.test','stream-r12.test']:
 print(name,sha(out/name),flush=True)
for name in ['internal/gen/rtgen/checked.go.tmpl','internal/gen/rtgen/rtgen.go','rt/instance.go','rt/batch.go','internal/gen/gogen/gogen.go','internal/gen/gogen/batch.go','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/standalone_bench_test.go','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/checked_bench_test.go','testdata/streaming/cocoon.lock.json','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/testdata/module.wasm']:
 print(name,sha(root/name),flush=True)
baseline=Path((out/'phase0-snapshot').read_text().strip())
print('baseline_snapshot',baseline,flush=True)
for name in ['rt/instance.go','internal/gen/gogen/gogen.go','testdata/streaming/cocoon.toml','testdata/streaming/cocoon.lock.json','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/testdata/module.wasm','testdata/streaming/go/streaming/standalone_bench_test.go']:
 print('baseline/'+name,sha(baseline/name),flush=True)
assert sha(baseline/'testdata/streaming/go/streaming/testdata/module.wasm')==sha(root/'testdata/streaming/go/streaming/testdata/module.wasm')
assert sha(baseline/'testdata/streaming/go/streaming/standalone_bench_test.go')==sha(root/'testdata/streaming/go/streaming/standalone_bench_test.go')
for key in ['GOGC','GOMEMLIMIT','GODEBUG']:
 print(key,env.get(key,'<unset>'),flush=True)
print('affinity',sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0)),flush=True)
def sample(binary,pattern,duration,name):
 args=[str(out/binary),'-test.run=^$','-test.bench='+pattern,'-test.benchtime='+duration,'-test.count=1','-test.benchmem']
 with (out/name).open('w') as log:
  log.write('# UTC '+datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()+'\n# '+' '.join(args)+'\n# initialGOMAXPROCS=16; explicit P leaf sets actual runtime procs\n');log.flush()
  subprocess.run(args,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
 measured_rows=0
 for row in (out/name).read_text().splitlines():
  fields=row.split()
  if not fields or not fields[0].startswith('BenchmarkStream') or 'ns/op' not in fields: continue
  measured_rows+=1
  match=re.search(r'/P([0-9]+)/',fields[0])
  expected=int(match[1]) if match else 16
  assert 'actual-procs' in fields, 'missing actual-procs: '+row
  actual=float(fields[fields.index('actual-procs')-1])
  assert actual==expected, f'wrong actual-procs {actual}, expected {expected}: {row}'
 assert not binary.startswith('stream') or measured_rows>0, 'no streaming rows: '+name
 print(name+' complete elapsed '+str(round(time.monotonic()-start,1))+'s',flush=True)
dd='^Benchmark(ObfuscateSQL|SketchAdd|SketchAddMany1k|ObfuscateTraces1k|SketchAddVaried|ObfuscateSQLVaried|SketchAddNegativeBins)$'
stand='^BenchmarkStreamStandalone$/^(Fixed|Holdout)$/^P(1|4|16)$/^Chunk(64|4096|16384)$/^Retain32$'
checked='^BenchmarkStreamChecked$/^(Fixed|Holdout)$/^P(1|4|16)$/^Chunk(64|4096|16384)$/^N32$/^(StandaloneHomesP|BatchHomes1|BatchHomesP)$'
length_stand='^BenchmarkStreamStandalone$/^Fixed$/^P16$/^Chunk(64|4096)$/^Retain(16|64|256)$'
length_checked='^BenchmarkStreamChecked$/^Fixed$/^P16$/^Chunk(64|4096)$/^N(16|64|256)$/^(StandaloneHomesP|BatchHomes1|BatchHomesP)$'
for rep in range(1,8):
 for label in (['r12','baseline'] if rep%2 else ['baseline','r12']): sample('stream-baseline-r12.test' if label=='baseline' else 'stream-r12.test',stand,'250ms',f'confirm-r12-stream-{label}-{rep}.txt')
print('INDEPENDENT STREAM CONFIRMATION COMPLETE elapsed '+str(round(time.monotonic()-start,1))+'s',flush=True)

=== internal/gen/rtgen/checked.go.tmpl ===
{{range .}}
{{if .Locked}}// Call runs a callback while holding the instance's guest execution lock.
{{else}}// checked runs the same per-operation boundary under a caller-held mu.
{{end}}func (i *Instance) {{.Name}}(op string, invoke func(call *Call) error) (err error) {
{{if .Locked}}	i.mu.Lock()
	defer i.mu.Unlock()
{{end}}	switch i.state {
	case instanceClosed:
		return ErrClosed
	case instancePoisoned:
		return ErrPoisoned
	case instanceOpen:
	}
	defer func() {
		if value := recover(); value != nil {
			i.poison()
			var message string
			if i.host != nil {
				message = i.host.takePanic()
			}
			err = Classify(op, value, message)
		}
	}()
	if i.host != nil {
		i.host.takePanic()
	}
	i.call = Call{instance: i}
	err = invoke(&i.call)
	if uint64(len(i.module.Memory())) > i.limits.MaxMemory {
		i.poison()
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w: linear memory exceeded maximum", op, ErrTooLarge)
	}
	if err != nil && errors.Is(err, ErrProtocol) {
		i.poison()
	}
	return err
}
{{end}}

=== internal/gen/rtgen/cmd/main.go ===
// Command rtgen reproduces the runtime's shared checked adapters.
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"

	"dario.cat/cocoon/internal/gen/rtgen"
)

func main() {
	err := generate()
	if err != nil {
		if _, writeErr := fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "rtgen:", err); writeErr != nil {
			os.Exit(1)
		}
		os.Exit(1)
	}
}

func generate() error {
	existing, err := os.ReadFile("instance.go")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("read runtime source: %w", err)
	}
	output, err := rtgen.Update(existing)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile("instance.go", output, 0o600); err != nil { // #nosec G703 -- Fixed repository path, independent of source contents.
		return fmt.Errorf("write checked boundary: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

=== internal/gen/rtgen/rtgen.go ===
// Package rtgen renders the shared per-operation runtime safety boundary.
package rtgen

import (
	"bytes"
	_ "embed"
	"fmt"
	"go/format"
	"go/scanner"
	"go/token"
	"strings"
	"text/template"
)

//go:embed checked.go.tmpl
var checkedTemplate string

// Generate emits locked and caller-held-lock adapters from one safety body.
func Generate() ([]byte, error) {
	source, err := template.New("checked").Parse(checkedTemplate)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse checked boundary: %w", err)
	}
	var output strings.Builder
	adapters := []struct {
		Name   string
		Locked bool
	}{{Name: "Call", Locked: true}, {Name: "checked"}}
	if renderErr := source.Execute(&output, adapters); renderErr != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("render checked boundary: %w", renderErr)
	}
	formatted, err := format.Source([]byte("package rt\n" + output.String()))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("format checked boundary: %w", err)
	}
	return bytes.TrimPrefix(formatted, []byte("package rt\n\n")), nil
}

// Update replaces only the marked adapters in an authored runtime source file.
func Update(existing []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	const start = "// BEGIN generated checked adapters"
	const end = "// END generated checked adapters"
	if bytes.Count(existing, []byte(start)) != 1 || bytes.Count(existing, []byte(end)) != 1 ||
		!bytes.Contains(existing, []byte("\n"+start+"\n")) || !bytes.Contains(existing, []byte("\n"+end+"\n")) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("expected exactly one checked adapter region")
	}
	if !commentMarkers(existing, start, end) {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checked adapter markers must be line comments")
	}
	prefix, rest, _ := bytes.Cut(existing, []byte(start))
	_, suffix, found := bytes.Cut(rest, []byte(end))
	if !found {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checked adapter markers are out of order")
	}
	body, err := Generate()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	output := append([]byte(nil), prefix...)
	output = append(output, start...)
	output = append(output, "\n\n"...)
	output = append(output, body...)
	output = append(output, end...)
	output = append(output, suffix...)
	formatted, err := format.Source(output)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("format runtime source: %w", err)
	}
	return formatted, nil
}

func commentMarkers(source []byte, start, end string) bool {
	file := token.NewFileSet().AddFile("instance.go", -1, len(source))
	var lexer scanner.Scanner
	lexer.Init(file, source, nil, scanner.ScanComments)
	var foundStart, foundEnd bool
	for {
		_, kind, literal := lexer.Scan()
		if kind == token.EOF {
			return foundStart && foundEnd
		}
		if kind == token.COMMENT {
			foundStart = foundStart || literal == start
			foundEnd = foundEnd || literal == end
		}
	}
}

=== internal/gen/rtgen/rtgen_test.go ===
package rtgen_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"go/ast"
	"go/format"
	"go/parser"
	"go/token"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"dario.cat/cocoon/internal/gen/rtgen"
)

func TestUpdateOnlyCheckedRegion(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	existing := []byte("package rt\nconst before=17\n// BEGIN generated checked adapters\nstale code\n// END generated checked adapters\nconst after=42\n")
	before := bytes.Clone(existing)
	updated, err := rtgen.Update(existing)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(existing, before) {
		t.Fatal("update changed its input")
	}
	text := string(updated)
	if strings.Contains(text, "stale code") || !strings.Contains(text, "const before = 17") || !strings.Contains(text, "const after = 42") {
		t.Fatal("update did not preserve authored surroundings")
	}
	again, err := rtgen.Update(updated)
	if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(updated, again) {
		t.Fatal("update is not reproducible", err)
	}
}

func TestUpdateRejectsAmbiguousSource(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	const start = "// BEGIN generated checked adapters\n"
	const end = "// END generated checked adapters\n"
	for name, source := range map[string]string{
		"missing":    "package rt\n" + start,
		"duplicate":  "package rt\n" + start + start + end,
		"reversed":   "package rt\n" + end + start,
		"embedded":   "package rt\n// not a marker " + start + end,
		"raw string": "package rt\nconst text = `\n" + start + end + "`\n",
		"block":      "package rt\n/*\n" + start + end + "*/\n",
		"invalid Go": "package broken {\n" + start + end,
	} {
		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
			t.Parallel()
			if output, err := rtgen.Update([]byte(source)); err == nil || output != nil {
				t.Fatal("invalid source must fail without output", err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestAdaptersShareEveryCheckedStatement(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	source, err := rtgen.Generate()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	file, err := parser.ParseFile(token.NewFileSet(), "instance.go", append([]byte("package rt\n"), source...), 0)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	bodies := make(map[string]string)
	for _, declaration := range file.Decls {
		method, ok := declaration.(*ast.FuncDecl)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if method.Name.Name == "Call" {
			if len(method.Body.List) < 2 {
				t.Fatal("missing locked adapter prefix")
			}
			var prefix strings.Builder
			for _, statement := range method.Body.List[:2] {
				if err := format.Node(&prefix, token.NewFileSet(), statement); err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				prefix.WriteByte('\n')
			}
			if prefix.String() != "i.mu.Lock()\ndefer i.mu.Unlock()\n" {
				t.Fatal("locked adapter must acquire and defer release of execution mutex")
			}
			method.Body.List = method.Body.List[2:]
		}
		var body strings.Builder
		if err := format.Node(&body, token.NewFileSet(), method.Body); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		bodies[method.Name.Name] = body.String()
	}
	if len(bodies) != 2 || bodies["Call"] == "" || bodies["Call"] != bodies["checked"] {
		t.Fatal("adapters must differ only by the execution lock prefix")
	}
}

=== rt/checked_gen_test.go ===
package rt_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"os"
	"testing"

	"dario.cat/cocoon/internal/gen/rtgen"
)

func TestCheckedBoundaryReproduces(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	actual, err := os.ReadFile("instance.go")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want, err := rtgen.Update(actual)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(actual, want) {
		t.Fatal("checked adapters drifted: update their shared template and run go generate ./rt")
	}
}

 print('PASS',gate,round(time.monotonic()-start,1),flush=True)
print('GATES COMPLETE',flush=True)

=== final-reproduce.py ===
import os, hashlib, json, subprocess
from pathlib import Path
root = Path.cwd()
out = root / '.cache/sessions-phase0'
env = dict(os.environ, GOTOOLCHAIN='local', CGO_ENABLED='0', GOCACHE=str(root/'.cache/go'), GOFLAGS='-buildvcs=false')
env['PATH'] = str(root/'.cache/binaryen/binaryen-version_133/bin')+':'+env['PATH']
roots = ['testdata/compute', 'examples/datadog', 'testdata/streaming', 'rt/instance.go']
paths = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','--',*roots],text=True).splitlines()
def fingerprints():
    return {p:hashlib.sha256((root/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths}
expected = fingerprints()
(out/'final-reproduce-before.json').write_text(json.dumps(expected,indent=2)+'\n')
with (out/'final-reproduce.log').open('w') as log:
    for number in (1,2):
        for manifest in ['testdata/compute/cocoon.toml','examples/datadog/cocoon.toml','testdata/streaming/cocoon.toml']:
            args=['/usr/bin/go','run','./cmd/cocoon','build','--manifest',manifest]
            print('Repeat',number,' '.join(args),file=log,flush=True)
            subprocess.run(args,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
        subprocess.run(['/usr/bin/go','generate','./rt'],env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
        actual=fingerprints()
        assert actual == expected, {p:(expected.get(p),actual.get(p)) for p in paths if expected.get(p)!=actual.get(p)}
        subprocess.run(['git','diff','--exit-code','HEAD','--',*roots],stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
        print('PASS: all',len(paths),'committed proof/runtime files reproduce byte-for-byte in repeat',number,file=log,flush=True)
(out/'final-reproduce-after.json').write_text(json.dumps(fingerprints(),indent=2)+'\n')
print('PASS: two repeated builds of all three proofs and runtime adapters; '+str(len(paths))+' files unchanged',flush=True)
